createMiddleware from \"next-intl/middleware\";\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport { routing } from \"./src/i18n/routing\";\nimport { defaultLocale, locales } from \"./src/i18n/locales\";\nimport { sanitizeReturnTo } from \"./src/lib/license-key-redirect\";\n\nconst intlMiddleware = createMiddleware(routing);\nconst localePattern = locales\n  .map((locale) => locale.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\\]\\\\]/g, \"\\\\$&\"))\n  .join(\"|\");\nconst LOCALE_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})(\\\\/|$)`);\nconst USER_ROUTE_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user`);\nconst USER_SIGN_IN_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user\\\\/sign-in`);\nconst USER_AUTH_SIGN_OUT_REGEX = new RegExp(\n  `^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user\\\\/auth\\\\/sign-out`,\n);\nconst USER_CUSTOMERS_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user\\\\/customers`);\n\nconst getPathLocale = (pathname: string) => {\n  const match = pathname.match(LOCALE_REGEX);\n  return match?.[1] ?? defaultLocale;\n};\n\n/**\n * Check if Better Auth session cookie is present.\n * For middleware, we only check cookie presence (no HTTP round-trip).\n * Actual session validation happens in the API layer.\n *\n * Better Auth prefixes cookies with \"__Secure-\" in production when\n * baseURL starts with \"https://\", so we check both names.\n */\nfunction hasSessionCookie(request: NextRequest): boolean {\n  return !!(\n    request.cookies.get(\"better-auth.session_token\")?.value ||\n    request.cookies.get(\"__Secure-better-auth.session_token\")?.value\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Get full Better Auth session via API call.\n * Only used for admin routes that need the user's email.\n */\nasync function getBetterAuthSession(request: NextRequest) {\n  const sessionCookie =\n    request.cookies.get(\"__Secure-better-auth.session_token\") ||\n    request.cookies.get(\"better-auth.session_token\");\n  if (!sessionCookie?.value) return null;\n\n  try {\n    const baseUrl = request.nextUrl.origin;\n    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/get-session`, {\n      headers: {\n        cookie: request.headers.get(\"cookie\") || \"\",\n      },\n    });\n\n    if (!response.ok) return null;\n\n    const data = await response.json();\n    return data?.user ?? null;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Middleware that handles:\n * 1. next-intl locale routing (adds locale prefix)\n * 2. Better Auth session checking\n * 3. User route protection (unified portal for customers and admins)\n */\nexport default async function middleware(request: NextRequest) {\n  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;\n\n  // Check if this is a user route (matches /<locale>/user/*)\n  const isUserRoute = USER_ROUTE_REGEX.test(pathname);\n  const isUserSignIn = USER_SIGN_IN_REGEX.test(pathname);\n  const isUserAuthSignOut = USER_AUTH_SIGN_OUT_REGEX.test(pathname);\n  const isUserCustomers = USER_CUSTOMERS_REGEX.test(pathname);\n\n  // =============================================================\n  // USER ROUTES - Unified portal for customers and admins\n  // =============================================================\n\n  // For /user/customers, require admin access\n  if (isUserCustomers) {\n    const user = await getBetterAuthSession(request);\n\n    // If not authenticated, redirect to user sign-in\n    if (!user) {\n      const locale = getPathLocale(pathname);\n      const signInUrl = new URL(`/${locale}/user/sign-in`, request.url);\n      signInUrl.searchParams.set(\"returnTo\", pathname);\n      return NextResponse.redirect(signInUrl);\n    }\n\n    // Must be admin to access customers page\n    if (user.role !== \"admin\") {\n      const locale = getPathLocale(pathname);\n      return NextResponse.redirect(\n        new URL(`/${locale}/user/dashboard`, request.url)\n      );\n    }\n\n    // User is authenticated and is admin - run intl middleware\n    const response = intlMiddleware(request) as NextResponse;\n    response.headers.set(\"x-pathname\", pathname);\n    return response;\n  }\n\n  // For other user routes (except sign-in, sign-out), check authentication via cookie presence\n  if (\n    isUserRoute &&\n    !isUserSignIn &&\n    !isUserAuthSignOut\n  ) {\n    if (!hasSessionCookie(request)) {\n      const locale = getPathLocale(pathname);\n      const signInUrl = new URL(`/${locale}/user/sign-in`, request.url);\n      signInUrl.searchParams.set(\"returnTo\", pathname);\n      return NextResponse.redirect(signInUrl);\n    }\n\n    // User has session cookie - run intl middleware\n    const response = intlMiddleware(request) as NextResponse;\n    response.headers.set(\"x-pathname\", pathname);\n    return response;\n  }\n\n  // For user sign-in page, check if already authenticated\n  if (isUserSignIn) {\n    if (hasSessionCookie(request)) {\n      const locale = getPathLocale(pathname);\n      const returnTo = request.nextUrl.searchParams.get(\"returnTo\");\n      const redirectUrl = sanitizeReturnTo(returnTo, `/${locale}/user/dashboard`);\n      return NextResponse.redirect(new URL(redirectUrl, request.url));\n    }\n\n    const response = intlMiddleware(request) as NextResponse;\n    response.headers.set(\"x-pathname\", pathname);\n    return response;\n  }\n\n  // For all other routes, just run intl middleware\n  const response = intlMiddleware(request) as NextResponse;\n  response.headers.set(\"x-pathname\", pathname);\n\n  return response;\n}\n\nexport const config = {\n  // Match all pathnames except for:\n  // - API routes (/api/*)\n  // - Raw model inventory endpoint (/models)\n  // - Next.js internal files (/_next/*)\n  // - Static files with extensions (*.*)\n  // - Documentation (/docs/*)\n  matcher: [\"/((?!api|models(?:/|$)|_next|_vercel|docs|.*\\\\..*).*)\"],\n};\n","numLines":161,"startLine":1,"totalLines":161}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fe6f7a40-584a-47db-92b8-0aeafa4b5b28","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"1404fe57-fd35-4903-8841-bfaeac9b6401","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"adbb75dd9ff4a13e2","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VkCiR5RdGk6J3V8yARKMwS","type":"tool_result","content":"1\texport const DEFAULT_LICENSE_KEY_REDIRECT = \"/en/user/dashboard\";\n2\t\n3\t/**\n4\t * Returns `returnTo` only when it is a same-origin, path-style redirect target;\n5\t * otherwise returns `fallback`. Rejects protocol-relative (\"//evil.com\") and\n * backslash (\"/\\\\evil.com\") prefixes that satisfy a naive `startsWith(\"/\")`\n * check but resolve to an attacker-controlled origin, preventing open redirects.\n */\nexport function sanitizeReturnTo(\n  returnTo: string | null | undefined,\n  fallback: string,\n) {\n  if (\n    returnTo &&\n    returnTo.startsWith(\"/\") &&\n    !returnTo.startsWith(\"//\") &&\n    !returnTo.startsWith(\"/\\\\\")\n  ) {\n    return returnTo;\n  }\n\n  return fallback;\n}\n\nexport function sanitizeLicenseKeyRedirect(callbackURL: string | undefined) {\n  return sanitizeReturnTo(callbackURL, DEFAULT_LICENSE_KEY_REDIRECT);\n}\n","numLines":28,"startLine":1,"totalLines":28}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1404fe57-fd35-4903-8841-bfaeac9b6401","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

// eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n55\t\n56\t  async function handleLicenseKeySignInWithKey(key: string) {\n57\t    setLicenseError(null);\n58\t    setLicenseSubmitting(true);\n59\t    try {\n60\t      const callbackURL = getCallbackURL();\n61\t      const res = await fetch(\"/api/auth/sign-in/license-key\", {\n62\t        method: \"POST\",\n63\t        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n64\t        body: JSON.stringify({ licenseKey: key.trim(), callbackURL }),\n65\t      });\n66\t      const data = await res.json();\n67\t      if (!res.ok) {\n68\t        setLicenseError(data.error ?? \"Sign-in failed. Admin features are shown based on\n * email being in the admin allowlist (checked in layout/middleware).\n */\nexport default function UserSignInPage() {\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const searchParams = useSearchParams();\n  const params = useParams();\n  const locale = (params.locale as string) || \"en\";\n\n  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<Tab>(\"license-key\");\n  const [autoSigningIn, setAutoSigningIn] = useState(false);\n\n  // License key tab state\n  const [licenseKey, setLicenseKey] = useState(\"\");\n  const [licenseSubmitting, setLicenseSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const [licenseError, setLicenseError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  // Email tab state\n  const [email, setEmail] = useState(\"\");\n  const [emailSubmitting, setEmailSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const [magicLinkSent, setMagicLinkSent] = useState(false);\n  const [emailError, setEmailError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  function getCallbackURL() {\n    const returnTo = searchParams.get(\"returnTo\");\n    return sanitizeReturnTo(returnTo, `/${locale}/user/dashboard`);\n  }\n\n  const licenseKeyParam = searchParams.get(\"licenseKey\");\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!licenseKeyParam) return;\n    setLicenseKey(licenseKeyParam);\n    setAutoSigningIn(true);\n    handleLicenseKeySignInWithKey(licenseKeyParam);\n  }, []); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n\n  async function handleLicenseKeySignInWithKey(key: string) {\n    setLicenseError(null);\n    setLicenseSubmitting(true);\n    try {\n      const callbackURL = getCallbackURL();\n      const res = await fetch(\"/api/auth/sign-in/license-key\", {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ licenseKey: key.trim(), callbackURL }),\n      });\n      const data = await res.json();\n      if (!res.ok) {\n        setLicenseError(data.error ?? \"Sign-in failed. Please try again.\");\n        setAutoSigningIn(false);\n      } else {\n        router.push(data.redirect);\n      }\n    } catch {\n      setLicenseError(\"An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.\");\n      setAutoSigningIn(false);\n    } finally {\n      setLicenseSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function handleLicenseKeySignIn(e?: React.FormEvent) {\n    e?.preventDefault();\n    await handleLicenseKeySignInWithKey(licenseKey);\n  }\n\n  async function handleSendMagicLink(e?: React.FormEvent) {\n    e?.preventDefault();\n    setEmailError(null);\n    setEmailSubmitting(true);\n\n    try {\n      const callbackURL = getCallbackURL();\n      const { error: authError } = await authClient.signIn.magicLink({\n        email,\n        callbackURL,\n      });\n\n      if (authError) {\n        setEmailError(authError.message ?? \"Failed to send magic link. Please try again.\");\n      } else {\n        setMagicLinkSent(true);\n      }\n    } catch {\n      setEmailError(\"An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.\");\n    } finally {\n      setEmailSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  const spinnerSvg = (\n    <svg className=\"animate-spin h-5 w-5\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\">\n      <circle className=\"opacity-25\" cx=\"12\" cy=\"12\" r=\"10\" stroke=\"currentColor\" strokeWidth=\"4\" />\n      <path className=\"opacity-75\" fill=\"currentColor\" d=\"M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z\" />\n    </svg>\n  );\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-900 via-slate-800 to-slate-900 py-12 px-4\">\n      <div className=\"max-w-md w-full\">\n        <div className=\"bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-lg rounded-2xl p-8 shadow-2xl border border-white/20\">\n          {/* Header */}\n          <div className=\"text-center mb-8\">\n            <h1 className=\"text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-2\">HyperWhisper</h1>\n            <p className=\"text-gray-400 text-sm\">\n              Sign in to view your licenses and credits\n            </p>\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Auto sign-in loading overlay */}\n          {autoSigningIn ? (\n            <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-8 gap-4\">\n              <svg className=\"animate-spin h-8 w-8 text-emerald-400\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\">\n                <circle className=\"opacity-25\" cx=\"12\" cy=\"12\" r=\"10\" stroke=\"currentColor\" strokeWidth=\"4\" />\n                <path className=\"opacity-75\" fill=\"currentColor\" d=\"M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z\" />\n              </svg>\n              <p className=\"text-gray-300 text-sm\">Signing you in…</p>\n            </div>\n          ) : (\n          <>\n\n          {/* Tab Switcher */}\n          <div className=\"flex rounded-lg bg-white/5 border border-white/10 p-1 mb-6\">\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              onClick={() => setActiveTab(\"license-key\")}\n              className={`flex-1 py-2 px-3 rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-all duration-200 ${\n                activeTab === \"license-key\"\n                  ? \"bg-white/15 text-white shadow-sm\"\n                  : \"text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-300\"\n              }`}\n            >\n              Account Key\n            </button>\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              onClick={() => setActiveTab(\"email\")}\n              className={`flex-1 py-2 px-3 rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-all duration-200 ${\n                activeTab === \"email\"\n                  ? \"bg-white/15 text-white shadow-sm\"\n                  : \"text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-300\"\n              }`}\n            >\n              Email\n            </button>\n          </div>\n\n          {/* License Key Tab */}\n          {activeTab === \"license-key\" && (\n            <form onSubmit={handleLicenseKeySignIn} className=\"space-y-6\">\n              {licenseError && (\n                <div className=\"p-4 bg-red-500/20 border border-red-500/30 rounded-lg\">\n                  <p className=\"text-red-300 text-sm text-center\">{licenseError}</p>\n                </div>\n              )}\n\n              <div>\n                <label htmlFor=\"license-key\" className=\"block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-2\">\n                  Account Key\n                </label>\n                <input\n                  id=\"license-key\"\n                  type=\"text\"\n                  required\n                  value={licenseKey}\n                  onChange={(e) => setLicenseKey(e.target.value)}\n                  className=\"w-full px-4 py-3 bg-white/5 border border-white/10 rounded-lg text-white placeholder-gray-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 focus:border-transparent (\n                        <span className=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-2\">\n                          {spinnerSvg}\n                          Sending...\n                        </span>\n                      ) : (\n                        \"Send Magic Link\"\n                      )}\n                    </button>\n\n                    <button\n                      type=\"button\"\n                      onClick={() => router.push(`/${locale}`)}\n                      className=\"w-full py-3 px-4 bg-white/5 border border-white/20 text-gray-400 font-medium rounded-lg hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-white transition-all duration-200\"\n                    >\n                      Back to Home\n                    </button>\n                  </div>\n                </form>\n              ) : (\n                // Magic Link Sent confirmation\n                <div className=\"space-y-6\">\n                  <div className=\"text-center\">\n                    <div className=\"mb-4\">\n                      <svg className=\"mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-emerald-400\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\" strokeWidth={1.5}>\n                        <path strokeLinecap=\"round\" strokeLinejoin=\"round\" d=\"M21.75 6.75v10.5a2.25 2.25 0 01-2.25 2.25h-15a2.25 2.25 0 01-2.25-2.25V6.75m19.5 0A2.25 2.25 0 0019.5 4.5h-15a2.25 2.25 0 00-2.25 2.25m19.5 0v.243a2.25 2.25 0 01-1.07 1.916l-7.5 4.615a2.25 2.25 0 01-2.36 0L3.32 8.91a2.25 2.25 0 01-1.07-1.916V6.75\" />\n                      </svg>\n                    </div>\n                    <p className=\"text-gray-300 mb-1\">We sent a magic link to</p>\n                    <p className=\"font-semibold text-white mb-4\">{email}</p>\n                    <p className=\"text-gray-400 text-sm\">\n                      Check your email and click the link to sign in. { createAuthEndpoint } from \"better-auth/api\";\nimport { setSessionCookie } from \"better-auth/cookies\";\nimport { eq } from \"drizzle-orm\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\n\nimport { findAccountByKey } from \"./db-layer\";\nimport { sanitizeLicenseKeyRedirect } from \"./license-key-redirect\";\n\nimport { db } from \"@/src/db\";\nimport { user } from \"@/src/db/schema/auth\";\n\nexport const licenseKeyPlugin = () => ({\n  id: \"license-key\",\n  endpoints: {\n    signInLicenseKey: createAuthEndpoint(\n      \"/sign-in/license-key\",\n      {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        requireHeaders: true,\n        body: z.object({\n          licenseKey: z.string(),\n          callbackURL: z.string().optional(),\n        }),\n      },\n      async (ctx) => {\n        const { licenseKey, callbackURL } = ctx.body;\n\n        const license = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey);\n\n        if (!license || license.status !== \"granted\") {\n          return ctx.json(\n            { error: \"Invalid or inactive license key.\" },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        if (!license.userId) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            {\n              error:\n                \"No account found for this license key. Please contact support.\",\n            },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        const [foundUser] = await db\n          .select()\n          .from(user)\n          .where(eq(user.id, license.userId))\n          .limit(1);\n\n        if (!foundUser) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            {\n              error:\n                \"No account found for this license key. Please contact support.\",\n            },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        const session = await ctx.context.internalAdapter.createSession(\n          foundUser.id,\n        );\n\n        if (!session) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            { error: \"Failed to create session.\" },\n            { status: 500 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        await setSessionCookie(ctx, { session, user: foundUser });\n\n        const ipAddress =\n          ctx.headers?.get(\"x-forwarded-for\")?.split(\",\")[0]?.trim() ??\n          \"unknown\";\n        const userAgent = ctx.headers?.get(\"user-agent\") ?? \"unknown\";\n        console.log(\n          `[license-key-sign-in] user=${foundUser.id} license=${license.id} ip=${ipAddress} ua=\"${userAgent}\"`,\n        );\n\n        return ctx.json({ redirect: sanitizeLicenseKeyRedirect(callbackURL) });\n      },\n    ),\n  },\n  rateLimit: [\n    {\n      pathMatcher(path: string) {\n        return path === \"/sign-in/license-key\";\n      },\n      window: 60,\n      max: 5,\n    },\n  ],\n});\n","numLines":98,"startLine":1,"totalLines":98}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b463e5b3-fafb-4afa-91b4-e770069c6f84","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"48bfaf44-12c0-4278-be79-2488dc586cc3","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"adbb75dd9ff4a13e2","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015xayTfBPGQpTQHfnsddQGK","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { redirect } from \"next/navigation\";\n2\t\n3\timport CreditsPurchase from \"@/components/credits/CreditsPurchase\";\n4\t\n5\t/**\n6\t * Credits Page\n7\t *\n8\t * Default (no params): the guest buy-credits flow — enter an email, pick an\n9\t * amount, and check out. The webhook mints a license key (the wallet) and emails\n10\t * it. See components/credits/CreditsPurchase.tsx.\n11\t *\n12\t * Native-app deep links still carry a key and route through sign-in so the user\n13\t * lands on their dashboard to top up an existing wallet:\n14\t *   - ?license_key=HW-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX (macOS, Windows)\n15\t *   - ?id=HW-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX          (legacy macOS — kept for older app versions)\n16\t */\n17\texport default async function CreditsPage({\n18\t  params,\n19\t  searchParams,\n20\t}: {\n21\t  params: Promise<{ locale: string }>;\n22\t  searchParams: Promise<{ id?: string; license_key?: string }>;\n23\t}) {\n24\t  const { locale } = await params;\n25\t  const { id, license_key } = await searchParams;\n26\t  const raw = id ?? license_key ?? \"\";\n27\t\n28\t  if (raw.startsWith(\"HW-\")) {\n29\t    redirect(\n30\t      `/${locale}/user/sign-in?licenseKey=${encodeURIComponent(raw)}&returnTo=/${locale}/user/dashboard`\n31\t    );\n32\t  }\n33\t\n34\t  return (\n35\t    <main className=\"min-h-screen bg-black px-6 py-24 md:py-28\">\n36\t      <CreditsPurchase locale={locale} />\n37\t    </main>\n38\t  );\n39\t}\n40\t"}]},"uuid":"54bede00-70ff-4a16-8ddb-bdb28d589cb6","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:47:10.217Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/app/[locale]/credits/page.tsx","content":"import { redirect } from \"next/navigation\";\n\nimport CreditsPurchase from \"@/components/credits/CreditsPurchase\";\n\n/**\n * Credits Page\n *\n * Default (no params): the guest buy-credits flow — enter an email, pick an\n * amount, and check out. The webhook mints a license key (the wallet) and emails\n * it. See components/credits/CreditsPurchase.tsx.\n *\n * Native-app deep links still carry a key and route through sign-in so the user\n * lands on their dashboard to top up an existing wallet:\n *   - ?license_key=HW-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX (macOS, Windows)\n *   - ?id=HW-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX          (legacy macOS — kept for older app versions)\n */\nexport default async function CreditsPage({\n  params,\n  searchParams,\n}: {\n  params: Promise<{ locale: string }>;\n  searchParams: Promise<{ id?: string; license_key?: string }>;\n}) {\n  const { locale } = await params;\n  const { id, license_key } = await searchParams;\n  const raw = id ?? license_key ?? \"\";\n\n  if (raw.startsWith(\"HW-\")) {\n    redirect(\n      `/${locale}/user/sign-in?licenseKey=${encodeURIComponent(raw)}&returnTo=/${locale}/user/dashboard`\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <main className=\"min-h-screen bg-black px-6 py-24 md:py-28\">\n      <CreditsPurchase locale={locale} />\n    </main>\n  );\n}\n","numLines":40,"startLine":1,"totalLines":40}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"48bfaf44-12c0-4278-be79-2488dc586cc3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

getBlogPost(slug);\n\n  if (!post) return {};\n\n  const title = post.frontMatter.title;\n  const description = post.frontMatter.description;\n  const canonical = `${BASE_URL}/en/blog/${post.slug}`;\n  const image = post.frontMatter.image\n    ? {\n        url: toAbsoluteUrl(post.frontMatter.image),\n        alt: post.frontMatter.imageAlt || title,\n      }\n    : null;\n\n  return {\n    title,\n    description,\n    alternates: {\n      canonical,\n    },\n    openGraph: {\n      type: \"article\",\n      locale: \"en_US\",\n      url: canonical,\n      title,\n      description,\n      images: image ? [image] : undefined,\n    },\n    twitter: {\n      card: \"summary_large_image\",\n      title,\n      description,\n      images: image ? [image.url] : undefined,\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nexport async function generateStaticParams() {\n  const posts = await getBlogPosts();\n  return posts.map((post) => ({ locale: \"en\", slug: post.slug }));\n}\n\nexport default async function BlogPostPage({ params }: Props) {\n  const { locale, slug } = await params;\n  if (locale !== \"en\") {\n    notFound();\n  }\n\n  const post = await getBlogPost(slug);\n  if (!post) {\n    notFound();\n  }\n\n  const publishedDate = formatDate(post.frontMatter.date);\n  const canonicalUrl = `${BASE_URL}/en/blog/${post.slug}`;\n  const jsonLd = {\n    \"@context\": \"https://schema.org\",\n    \"@type\": \"Article\",\n    headline: post.frontMatter.title,\n    description: post.frontMatter.description,","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a8dd142f-5025-453b-a93b-767b9619423f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

The real defect in this area is the *sitemap* claiming the blog exists in 40 locales (reported above), not the revalidation.\n- **`nextjs/i18n/` directory** — confirmed it does not exist; only the file `nextjs/i18n.ts`. Since next-intl v4 only probes `./i18n/request.*` and `./src/i18n/request.*` (never `./i18n.ts`, which was the v2-era path), `src/i18n/request.ts` is unambiguously the live config. The lead held, and I confirmed the resolution order directly from the installed plugin source rather than from docs.\n- **Unprefixed absolute URLs in emails and Stripe redirects** (`https://hyperwhisper.com/user/dashboard`, `success_url: ${siteUrl}/purchase-success?...`, `cancel_url: ${siteUrl}/credits`, `return_url: ${siteUrl}/user`, `app/api/checkout/route.ts` → `/credits`) are **not** I14 violations. They omit the locale rather than hardcoding `en`, so the next-intl middleware resolves them against the user's `NEXT_LOCALE` cookie / `Accept-Language`. They cost one extra 307 but preserve the user's language.\n- **`components/language-switcher.tsx`** — clean; uses `usePathname`/`useRouter` from `@/src/i18n/navigation` with `router.replace(pathname, { locale })`, iterating the full `locales` array.
